# Project Status

This file is a living document. Update it as decisions are made or phases shift.

---

## Current Phase

**Phase 2 — Single-company browsing UI**

Goal: a usable Overview page and Time Slicer detail view for one company, single-period mode first. Range and Compare modes are deferred to Phase 3.

HTTP-verified live: `/company/APPL` → 404 with AAPL suggestion link
- **Defect-hunt round 13 (2026-09-30):** fresh tickers KO + ABBV + MS + NEE + AMT seeded. **(1)** Filer-mislabeled 10-K Q4 stubs — ABBV's 10-K tags its 91-day Q4 `Revenues` stub fp="FY" (verified against raw SEC JSON); the phantom FY row stole a quarter slot (ABBV and AMT seeded 7 quarters) and collided on (cik, period_end) at upsert. New `xbrl._merge_standalone_with_q4()` relabels the stub Q4 (filer's fact supersedes the derived duplicate) in all three revenue paths — generic, `_finance_total_revenue`, `_bank_quarterly_revenue` (where it would otherwise have been *summed* with the derived Q4). ABBV/AMT re-seeded with 8 clean quarters; ABBV Q4 FY2025 EPS now the filer's 1.02/1.02 (the pre-fix seed's −0.0124/−0.0122 loss-quarter inversion is gone too). **(2)** Total-equity chain now prefers `StockholdersEquityIncludingPortionAttributableToNoncontrollingInterest` — NEE files both tags fresh and parent-only left a $10.3B Assets = Liabilities + Equity gap (154.79 + 55.22 ≠ 221.42); the total tag closes it exactly (154.79 + 66.63 = 221.42). NEE re-seeded, identity holds every quarter. MS clean (8 quarters, FCF correctly suppressed); KO clean (8 quarters, identity holds)
- **Range mode — metric × quarters tables (PRD §4.2, second slice, 2026-09-30):** when the detail view's From/To selectors narrow the chart window to 2+ quarters, the tabbed Income / Cash Flow / Balance Sheet tables switch from the single-selected-period view to a metric × quarters grid (`DetailContext.range_table`, values straight off the stored rows, no YoY/QoQ columns — the window is the comparison). Full-history and one-quarter windows keep the period view; yearly mode unaffected; KPI cards / press-release highlights / downloads still follow the selected period. `range_grid` Jinja macro (horizontally scrollable); 4 new service tests. HTTP-verified live on KO (Q4 FY2024 → Q2 FY2025 grid values match the DB; default view unchanged)
- **Defect-hunt fixes (2026-09-28):** unknown ticker now renders a friendly 404 page instead of a 500 (`_resolve_cik` converts EDGAR `KeyError` → `CompanyNotFoundError`); Wikipedia enrichment tolerates `httpx.InvalidURL` so a broken proxy config no longer 500s refresh; transient Yahoo quote failures no longer wipe a stored `last_price`/`market_cap` (never-wipe policy, matching risk factors / press-release highlights); **batch 2:** bracketed IPv6 literals in `no_proxy` (injected by the runtime) crashed *every* `httpx.Client()` construction — fixed at the root via `data/proxy_env.py::sanitize_proxy_env()` on package import, instead of swallowing the exception per call site; **batch 3:** XOM comparative-label collision fixed — when a CIK's dataset lacks the original filing, comparative columns inherit the later filing's `fy` and duplicate selector options; `xbrl._dedupe_period_labels` now enforces unique (fy, fp) labels at extraction (earlier end walks its year back; latest end keeps its label); **batch 5 (2026-09-29):** NVDA CapEx tag abandonment fixed — NVDA stopped filing `PaymentsToAcquirePropertyPlantAndEquipment` after 2020 and reports CapEx as `PaymentsToAcquireProductiveAssets`; the CAPEX fallback chain now includes both (freshness wins per the PLUG staleness rule), restoring FCF for all 8 NVDA quarters and the valuation-card FCF-yield cross-check; **batch 6 (2026-09-29):** press-release exhibit discovery generalized — `_find_ex99_doc` only matched `ex99`-style filenames, so NVDA/META/GOOGL/TSLA/AMD/JNJ/RDDT (which name exhibits `q2fy27pr.htm`-style, no "ex99" anywhere) silently got zero press-release highlights; new `_pick_release_doc()` also matches `*pressrelease*` / `*earningsrelease*` / `-pr`/`_pr` suffixes / quarter-styled `…pr` stems with a digit (excludes `proper.htm` lookalikes), ex99 keeps priority; **batch 7 (2026-09-29):** bank revenue composite — JPM seeded with 2013–2014 quarters because banks file quarterly revenue as `NoninterestIncome` + `InterestIncomeExpenseNet` while the generic `Revenues` chain holds only annual facts (JPM's quarterly `Revenues` stops in 2014; WFC's in 2020; GS files none), and revenue is the canonical anchor so *every* joined metric (net income, EPS) was pinned a decade stale; finance SICs (6000–6299) now use a composite revenue series summing both components (verified exact match to quarterly `Revenues` on BAC 2026 Q1/Q2, diff 0); JPM/WFC re-seeded with current quarters; **batch 8 (2026-09-29):** finance `Revenues` preference — MET (insurer, SIC 6331) seeded with $0.7B revenue because the generic chain prefers the `RevenueFromContractWithCustomerExcludingAssessedTax` tag, which for insurers is a fee-income sub-component, while the filer's own `Revenues` ($19B total) sat later in the chain; finance SICs (6000–6999) now prefer quarterly `Revenues` when absolutely current (≤180 days from today since batch 9's threshold change — a *relative* check can't work since JPM files nothing else quarterly in the chain), with the bank composite as fallback for stale/missing `Revenues`. **batch 9 (2026-09-29):** REIT net-income tag abandonment — Realty Income (O) stopped filing quarterly `NetIncomeLoss` after 2025-Q3 (last quarterly fact 273 days behind the replacement tag), so net income showed `None` for 2026 Q1/Q2 while EPS was present; the `NET_INCOME` chain now falls back to `NetIncomeLossAvailableToCommonStockholdersBasic` then `ProfitLoss`, and the tag-abandonment staleness threshold tightened 400→180 days (two filing cycles — the relative rule compares same-filer tags, so slow filers are never penalized); O re-seeded with ni=$311.8M/$344.0M. **batch 9 (2026-09-29):** LLY missing `Liabilities` tag — Eli Lilly never files a standalone `Liabilities` tag (only `LiabilitiesAndStockholdersEquity` + `StockholdersEquity`), so Total Liabilities showed "—" in the balance-sheet tab and compare table; missing instants are now derived from the accounting identity (Assets − Equity, same filing, only where both components exist; explicit filings never overwritten) — verified 108.4B = 142.3 − 33.9 on live LLY. **batch 10 (2026-09-29):** instant-fact staleness blind spot — `_newest_in_window` ignored facts without a `start`, so the 180-day tag-abandonment rule never engaged for balance-sheet metrics and the first chain tag always won even when abandoned years ago; UNH showed Total Equity "—" for all 8 quarters (`StockholdersEquity` abandoned 2015, fresh `…IncludingPortionAttributableToNoncontrollingInterest` ignored) and PG showed Cash & Equivalents "—" for all 8 quarters (`CashAndCashEquivalentsAtCarryingValue` abandoned 2019); a `None` window now counts facts of any duration, and both metrics render (PG identity holds exactly: 72.2+54.3=126.5). **batch 10 (2026-09-29):** GS Q2 2026 basic EPS blank — the 10-Q filed only `EarningsPerShareDiluted`; per-quarter fallback now fills missing basic-EPS ends from diluted (basic still preferred where present; differs by fractions of a percent) — Q2 2026 now $20.98 live. **batch 11 (2026-09-29):** LLY CapEx tag discovery — Eli Lilly never filed `PaymentsToAcquirePropertyPlantAndEquipment` and abandoned `PaymentsToAcquireProductiveAssets` in 2022; it files "Capital expenditures" as `PaymentsToAcquireOtherPropertyPlantAndEquipment`, so FCF showed "—" for all 8 quarters; the CAPEX chain now includes that tag (freshness rule picks it; NVDA precedent) — LLY FCF now $0.16B–$7.76B across quarters, Overview card renders $7.76B. **batch 11 (2026-09-29):** 52/53-week 9M window — Costco's 36-week 9M YTD cash-flow fact is 251 days, below the old 255-day floor (the Q4-derivation path used 250), so COST's Q3 FCF could not be uncumulated and showed "—"; both 9M windows unified at a 240-day floor — Q3 FY2026 FCF now $2.04B, all 8 quarters present. **batch 11 (2026-09-29):** missing-equity identity derivation — `_derive_missing_liabilities` generalized to `_derive_missing_balance_sheet`: filers with no standalone equity tag (Visa files only Assets + Liabilities) now get Total Equity = Assets − Liabilities (V: $35.66B); assets is never derived; explicitly filed values never overwritten — AMD/WMT/AMZN re-seeded and their liabilities now derive too ($17.24B/$195.68B/$544.07B). Fresh tickers AMZN + CAT seeded clean (CAT all metrics present; AMZN liabilities derived, FCF −$8.8B honest). **batch 12 (2026-09-30):** Q4 EPS derivation fixed — per-share values were un-cumulated by subtraction (FY_EPS − 9M_EPS) even though the two figures divide by different share counts; DUOL's FY2025 Q4 rendered diluted $0.94 *above* basic $0.88 (arithmetically impossible). `_derive_q4_income` now derives Q4 EPS as Q4 net income over the implied Q4 share count ((12·FY_avg − 9·9M_avg)/3 from the filed NI/EPS pairs), falling back to precision-rounded subtraction when NI facts are missing/zero — DUOL now $0.90 basic > $0.89 diluted; residual sub-cent flips from filing-rounded inputs (UBER FY2024 Q4) documented as a limitation. **batch 12 (2026-09-30):** FCF suppressed for all finance SICs (6000–6999) — SoFi files a CapEx tag so it rendered a misleading −$3.99B FCF while every bank/insurer/REIT peer shows "—"; OpCF for lenders is dominated by balance-sheet flows, so the metric is not meaningful. Fresh tickers SOFI + DUOL + UBER + RBLX seeded clean. **Data-source limits (not defects):** Visa files no EPS tags at all in companyfacts (per-share-class EPS uses dimensional facts, which companyfacts excludes) — EPS shows "—" by design; REIT/bank/insurer FCF stays "—" (no CapEx-line filing / not a meaningful metric) — banks (BAC/JPM/WFC), MET, O, XOM single-filing YTD gaps **Environmental caveat:** SEC Archives document fetches currently 403 ("Undeclared Automated Tool") while index listings + JSON API work — discovery finds the right exhibits but downloads are blocked; enrichment is best-effort and self-heals on future refreshes. **Data-source limits (not defects):** Visa files no EPS tags at all in companyfacts (per-share-class EPS uses dimensional facts, which companyfacts excludes) — EPS shows "—" by design

| 2026-10-06 | Batch 21 (fresh-ticker defect hunt — FY-label collision arbitration + unsupported-filer message): hunt on ORCL (May year-end), MU, MRNA, SPG, F, ASML. (1) **fix** `_dedupe_period_labels` (`data/xbrl.py`) resolved (fy, fp) collisions by trusting the *later* end's label — but a filer's newest filing can itself mislabel the current quarter's fy (Oracle's Sep-2026 10-Q tags Q1 FY2027 as fy=2026; Salesforce's Jan 10-K tags Q4 FY2026 as fy=2025), so the old rule corrupted the *correct* rows (ORCL's Q1 FY2026 → "FY2025 Q1", CRM's Q4 FY2025 → "FY2024 Q4"). Now sequence-consistency arbitrates: on collision the earlier end keeps the label when its claim matches the quarter-sequence expectation, and the later end is relabeled to its expectation; legacy later-keeps rule only when there is no sequence context; collision-free rows untouched. ORCL + CRM re-seeded from SEC — all quarters sequence-consistent. (2) **fix** ASML (EUR-only revenue, 20-F forms) hard-failed with bare `KeyError: 'No XBRL facts found for metric revenue'`; now `UnsupportedFilerError` with a plain-language reason ("reports in EUR; files 20-F, 20-F/A instead of 10-Q/10-K — Tickerlens covers US-GAAP filers reporting in USD with quarterly 10-Q/10-K filings."), rendered cleanly on the 404 page by the overview/vs/tearsheet first-visit paths. No template change (backend-only). Suite at 358 passing (6 new). Live: /company/{ORCL,CRM,MU} + detail 200 with corrected labels; /company/ASML → 404 with the friendly message. MU's $1045 quote verified against market data (memory supercycle, +458% 1Y) — real, not a quote defect. |

(2) **robustness** `yf.Ticker.info` had no socket timeout — a stalled Yahoo connection could block a caller forever; new `_fetch_info()` wrapper enforces a 15s hard timeout (shared 4-worker pool) and converts executor-shutdown `RuntimeError` to `TimeoutError` so background warmers can't print tracebacks during teardown; `get_quote`/`get_day_change_pct`/cache entry points keep their never-raise contracts. ---

## Phase roadmap (reference)

- **Phase 0:** Setup + EDGAR exploration (weeks 0–1)
- **Phase 1:** Data flowing for one company (weeks 1–4)
- **Phase 2:** Single-company browsing UI (weeks 5–8)
- **Phase 3:** Scale to all US public companies + watchlist + downloads (weeks 9–14)
- **Phase 4:** Earnings calendar + alerts (weeks 15–18)
- **Phase 5:** AI analysis (weeks 19–24)
- **Phase 6:** News feed (weeks 25–28)

Each phase has explicit "done" criteria — don't jump ahead.
| 2026-10-06 | Batch 18 (8-K disclosure integration audit — 5 defects fixed): the 8-K guidance/transcript feature shipped 2026-10-05 but rendered "Not available for this period" for most tickers — audit against real data (NVDA/TSLA/JPM/AAPL/MSFT) found and fixed five defects. (1) **fix** stale-ticker backfill gap — `enrich_company` only runs on first fetch, so tickers fetched before 2026-10-05 never got disclosures; `get_detail` now lazily backfills missing press-release highlights/guidance/transcript (best-effort, never raises, commits only on its own session; short-circuits + disk-cached SEC responses keep repeat visits cheap). (2) **fix** 8-K matching window — `_match_8k` anchored on the 10-Q/10-K *filing* date with a 21-day window, systematically missing annual periods (JPM's 10-K filed 31 days after its earnings 8-K) and borderline 10-Qs (23-day gap); now anchors on the period end (first Item 2.02 8-K within 60 days after report date, nearest the formal filing). JPM matches went 2/4 → 4/4. (3) **fix** supplement-vs-narrative exhibit pick — `_pick_release_doc` picked JPMorgan's `*erfex992supplement.htm` (3.3MB of tables, no guidance/quotes) over `*exhibit991narrative.htm`; "supplement" filenames now lose ties to the narrative release. (4) **fix** speaker-first executive quotes — `_QUOTE_RE` only matched `"…" said X`; JPM's `Dimon, Chairman and CEO, commented [on the financial results]: "…"` format (incl. words between verb and colon) now matches too. (5) **fix** non-calendar-FY quarter labels — `_assign_fy_and_quarter` counted quarters forward from Q1 within each fy group, mislabeling partial oldest years (NVDA's 2024-10-27 10-Q labeled "Q1 FY2025"); quarters before the group's 10-K now count back from Q4. Also: enrichment refill is per-field never-overwrite (a row with highlights but no guidance gets guidance filled without touching highlights). Live-verified on :8123: NVDA 8/8 quarters fully populated (guidance renders NVIDIA's real $108.0B Q3 FY2027 outlook), JPM 4/4 discoverable quarters have press + transcript (guidance correctly None — banks don't guide), 6/6 route checks 200. Suite at 344 passing (14 new). |

Known tiers keep the original phrasing (verified "Evaluated as a Mid-Cap filer…" live on DUOL). No template change (backend-only copy fix). Suite at 346 passing (2 new). |

| 2026-10-07 | Batch 25 (AI fundamental-analysis integration audit — 4 defects fixed): audited `generate_ai_analysis` against live data for all 58 DB companies. (1) **fix** solvency ladder miscalibrated for structurally-levered sectors — the industrial assets/liabilities ladder scored every bank 40/100 (JPM 1.08x, BAC 1.09x, GS 1.06x, MS 1.08x, WFC 1.09x, MET 1.04x), reading deposit-funded balance sheets as distress, dragging each bank's composite down ~5 pts and flipping MET's signal to Avoid (46) on a healthy insurer. New `_solvency_score()` is sector-aware (financials: bank/securities/insurance; plus REITs with a milder ladder); industrials unchanged; 40-point floor still catches genuinely thin sheets. MET now Watch (51), JPM 80→87, all banks +5–7 composite points. (2) **fix** briefing silently contradicted the valuation card — TSLA rendered "Watch" 66/100 next to a Strong Sell −97.6% model target on the same page. | 2026-10-09 | Batch 37 (EDGAR auto-refresh freshness audit — 2 defects fixed): audited all 78 tracked tickers against live EDGAR submissions: 6 have filed 10-Qs newer than stored data (V, KO, NEE, PYPL, F, DUK — all missing Q2 2026). Root cause of the missing quarters is SEC-side: SEC's companyfacts API has not ingested those 10-Qs' XBRL facts (verified live, uncached — e.g. V's 2026-07-29 10-Q ships a 408KB xbrl.zip yet companyfacts' newest V facts are filed 2026-04-29), so no app pull could have landed them. But the audit exposed two real app defects in the freshness machinery. **fix** (`data/edgar.py`): the EDGAR disk cache was write-once, read-forever — no TTL — so `refresh_financials_if_stale`'s trigger could go blind to new filings and a triggered `fetch_and_persist` re-extracted the same stale snapshot forever. Cache now has a 6h TTL (matching `_FILINGS_CHECK_TTL`); `submissions()`/`companyfacts()` gain `fresh=True` to bypass it. **fix** (`services/financials.py`): the `xbrl_filings_processed` marker was set to the latest filing date even when that filing's quarter wasn't extracted — DUK's marker sat at 2026-08-04 (its Q2 10-Q filing date) while stored data ended 2026-03-31, so the trigger could never fire again and the quarter would be skipped until the next quarter's filing. New `_processed_filing_marker()`: the marker advances only to filings whose `reportDate` quarter is present in the extracted rows, so the check keeps retrying every 6h until SEC ingests the facts. Deliberate pulls now always read live EDGAR. One-time remediation: DUK's persisted marker reset 2026-08-04 → 2026-05-05 (its latest processed filing). 7 new tests (4 cache TTL incl. fresh-kwarg bypass; 3 marker semantics incl. DUK-shape regression + refire loop). Suite at 417 green. When the primary search yields nothing relevant, the batch retries with the suffix stripped (`_strip_corp_suffix`: trailing /MA/ state tags + one suffix token) and accepts only an article *named* the stripped name (order-insensitive word-set equality, suffix-stripped both sides, never-relevant exclusions kept) — so "Applied Digital Data Systems" (a 1969 terminal maker) is *declined* for APLD rather than misattributed (batch-47 IBM/Commodore class). | 2026-10-13 | Batch 65 (Q4-EPS derivation from the continuing-operations annual tag — HAL class closed): no new seeds this batch; the batch-63 follow-up got a full investigation instead. **Finding:** Halliburton's 10-Ks tag no annual EPS under the generic `EarningsPerShareDiluted` tag (last annual fact FY2019), but they DO tag the annual under `IncomeLossFromContinuingOperationsPerDilutedShare` (FY2025 $1.50, FY2024 $2.83 via the corrected comparative twin) — batch 64's "no FY anchor" close checked only the generic tag, so it missed the anchor. Its 10-Qs tag quarters + 9M under the generic tag, so Q4 was underivable and HAL stored two EPS-less Q4 rows, forcing the valuation onto the sales path. **fix** (`data/xbrl.py`): the EPS branch of `quarterly_income_metric` now fetches the continuing-operations per-share tag as an annual-only fallback, threaded winner-only into `_derive_q4_income`; a fallback annual is used only for starts the primary tag lacks, only with the NI pair present, and only when the implied-shares coherence check passes at 5% (FY shares from NI/EPS vs 9M shares) — a filer with material discontinued operations is rejected fail-closed, and if the share-implied derivation returns None on the fallback path the row is skipped (no plain subtraction, which would mix continuing-ops annual with total 9M). The primary path gains the same check at a loose 75% as a tripwire against scale-corrupted inputs (HAL's 2022–2024 10-Qs tagged net-income dollar amounts, in thousands, under the EPS tag — a 1e6× mis-scale that would print a Q4 EPS wrong by ~4×); 75% sits above the worst legitimate divergence observed DB-wide (Capital One FY2025, 56%, Discover merger + acquisition accounting — first cut at 10% regressed COF and SOFI to EPS-less Q4s, caught by the DB-wide diff, tolerance raised with a COF-shaped regression test locking the old subtraction value). Loss years abstain and keep existing behavior. Blast radius verified by DB-wide before/after extraction over all 168 companies (git-stash A/B, same 6h-cached companyfacts): exactly ONE ticker changes — HAL gains Q4 FY2024 EPS (basic $0.71, diluted $0.6999) and Q4 FY2025 EPS ($0.6922/$0.6922); COF, SOFI, and all other 165 are byte-identical. | 2026-10-16 | Batch 76 (serve-stale-on-error for the EDGAR cache — closing the batch-75 incident follow-up): the batch-75 incident (server 500'd then stalled ~10 min when `.edgar_cache`'s company_tickers.json expired and the live re-fetch timed out through the proxy) was the last structural gap in the never-raise pattern. **fix** `EdgarClient.fetch_json` and `fetch_text` (`src/tickerlens/data/edgar.py`): on any live-fetch failure (timeout, connection error, non-2xx) the stale on-disk snapshot is served instead of raising — a hours-old ticker→CIK map resolves CIKs fine and filing-doc URLs are immutable so stale copies are identical content. **feature** per-URL failure cooldown (`FAILURE_COOLDOWN_SECONDS = 5 min`): after a failed live fetch, subsequent requests serve stale immediately without another 30s network attempt — this is what kills the ~10-min serial-timeout stall, not just the 500s. Success clears the failure record so the live fetch is retried once the cooldown lapses (no manual re-warm); with no cache on disk the exception still propagates (honest cold-boot failure). `fresh=True` freshness-trigger calls now return the existing snapshot instead of raising → "no new filings", the safe honest answer under an outage. 6 new tests (stale-on-connect-error, stale-on-503, no-cache-still-raises, cooldown-skips-retry, recovery-retries-live, fetch_text stale). | 2026-10-20 | Batch 100 (GS cover-page title-block extraction — the batch-97 carried follow-up, 1 defect class fixed): **Finding:** Goldman Sachs opens its ex-99.1 with a cover page (quarter-period title + "Earnings Results", inline media/investor contacts, masthead + address, repeated banner) before the real headline — the naive pick took the cover title ("Second Quarter 2026") and the lede filled with contact/masthead lines; worse, 3 recent quarters stored the EX-99.2 *presentation deck* (brand-color junk) from an older discovery ranking. **fix** (`data/filings.py`): new `_cover_block_headline` — narrowly triggered (period title + kind line, then a Media/Investor-Relations contact line carrying a phone number and a banner repeating the period words, all within 12 lines), skips the whole cover block; the real headline often wraps mid-phrase ("...Per Common" / "Share of $20.98 ...") so continuation lines join here with a section-heading stop (a fragment-shaped candidate is the "Financial Summary" table heading, not headline; $-/%-bearing fragments never stop since real continuations carry figures). `_match_cover_title` handles all four observed wraps: single-line, "Full Year and"/"Fourth Quarter 2025", "Full Year"/"and"/"Fourth Quarter 2024", "Third Quarter"/"2024". Also `_looks_like_table_heading` folds a short table heading ("Book Value Per Share") into `_drop_table_fragment_runs` so a heading + its last row drops as one run instead of leaking 3 stray lines into the lede. 9 new regression tests (4 cover shapes incl. wrapped titles + title-case banner, 3 no-fire negatives, section-heading stop, table-heading fold + 2-fragment-run keep). Suite 856 green (847 + 9), `git diff --check` clean. **Live backfill:** cleared GS's 6 stored rows (3 deck-junk + 3 weak) and re-ran — GS 6/8 → 8/8 with real headlines (the 2 previously-None quarters rescued too: Q3 FY2025 "$12.25", Q2 FY2025 "$10.91", cross-checked against the Q2-2026 release's comparatives); spot-checked figures against the real exhibits. FOURTH QUARTER 2024 RESULTS", "GE Vernova reports second quarter 2025 financial results" — GEV's opens with a footnote-definition line, noted wart). ## Batch 97 (2026-10-19) — partial-highlight forensic sweep: 60 → 12

**Forensics:** classified 134 residual quarters across the 60 partial companies — 59 no-period (discovery never returned the quarter), 40 discovery-gap (8-K found, no release doc), 28 extract-gap (doc found, extraction failed), 7 no-8k.

**Discovery fixes (`services/ir_download.py`):**
- 10 new exhibit-naming conventions in `_pick_release_doc` (ACN bare `8-kexhibit`, AFRM `shareholderlett` truncation, CART `sha/shar` + ordinal-word guard, CCI month+year+`release`, CTVA reversed-quarter `4q` + `schedules` supplement penalty, DAL `announces`+month, EQIX loose `pr`+junk with date/quarter guard, GPC truncated `earn`+quarter, MKC bare `reports`, UNH `991`-leading-letter tier 0).
- **Paginated submissions:** `EdgarClient.submissions_bucket()` + `_merge_filings_recent()` — `discover_earnings_filings` now pages `filings.files` buckets until it has n_quarters 10-Q/10-K (BAC's recent bucket held only 3 10-Q + 1 10-K — the 59 no-period quarters). Bucket JSON top-level IS the recent-shaped block.
- **Tier-0 99.2/99.3 exclusion:** 99.2+ supplements (BAC `ex993`, MET `ex992`) never outrank the 99.1 release; undotted `ex9901` (CDNS) still matches via `(?!\d|\.\d)` guard (allows `.htm` dot, blocks `.2`/`.3`).
- **`_lone_exhibit_fallback`:** single-exhibit 8-Ks led by 2.02 (TSLA `exhibit.htm`, PNC `a2025_11x07xbaabxvf.htm`, HAL `livemastererdocument.htm`, UNP `ex_713315.htm`) — guard is "2.02 first item", not "exactly 2.02" (most earnings 8-Ks carry 9.01).
- **`_match_8k` guards:** candidates must be filed ≥7d after period end (TSLA's 2-day deliveries 8-K, REGN's 6-day no-exhibit 8-K) and ≤ the 10-Q/10-K filing date (PNC's post-10-Q conference 8-K, FDX's recast-supplement 8-K); nearest to filing date wins. Fixed 3 wrong-content quarters (PNC×2 conference decks, FDX recast supplement) — cleared and re-filled.
- **`oldest_period_end` + 8-K pagination:** discovery extends past n_quarters to cover the oldest stored period (V's 2024-06-30 was the 9th filing — stored window lagged submissions via EDGAR companyfacts delay) and pages until 8-Ks reach the oldest period's window (MS's Oct-2024 8-K sat in an older bucket than its Nov-2024 10-Q).

**Extraction fixes (`data/filings.py`):** bare `EX-99` admin line (NEE), `Page X of Y` (MET), date lines (LLY), `For release: Immediately` variant; financial-table guard rejects table-shaped "Highlights" sections (LLY, WM); highlights heading tightened to start-or-end (WM footnote verb); lede table-fragment drop (MET) + footnote skip (WM); `_skip_contact_block` index bug.

**Results:** 48 quarters rescued (60→12 partial, 192→~228 at 8/8). Honest Nones by design: EOG (supplement-only ex-99.1), AFRM (image-only letter), LIN (5-line stub), SKYD (never files 2.02). Out-of-scope findings: XOM mapped to wrong CIK (SEC ticker map points to holdings entity — seeding defect); SHOP is a 6-K foreign filer (no 10-Q for 2024-09-30); GS cover-page layout needs a title-block-skip heuristic.

**Tests:** 31 new (26 discovery/extraction + 5 matcher/pagination). Full suite 829 green, `git diff --check` clean.

**Carried follow-ups:** HAL business-vs-financial highlights section priority; GS cover-page extraction; XOM CIK re-seed; SHOP 6-K discovery (foreign filers).

### Batch 102 — financial bullets win over business "Highlights" sections (2026-10-20)
Halliburton's releases open with financial bullets ("Net income of $0.64 per diluted share") right after the headline, then carry a later "Selective Technology & Highlights" section of business-PR bullets (product launches, partnerships). The extractor used to return the first "Highlights" heading match, burying the figures. `_extract_highlights_section` now collects ALL candidates (leading bullet block + every "Highlights" section) and picks the one densest with reported financial figures (≥2 figure lines, ≥40% of lines financial); ties keep the earliest — the pre-batch-102 behavior when nothing is financial. `_leading_bullet_block` handles wrapped bullet continuations (HAL Q2 2025's "... free cash flow1 of / approximately $582 million."), stopping at the dateline paragraph (item closed + non-bullet next line). Live backfill: HAL 8/8 quarters now lead with financial bullets (Q2 2025 rescued from business-only by the wrap fix).

**Tests:** 6 new (financial priority, wrapped bullets, threshold, fallbacks). Full suite 871 green, `git diff --check` clean. Live: /company/HAL, /detail, /tearsheet, / all 200; detail renders the rescued financial bullets.

**Carried follow-ups:** SHOP 6-K discovery (foreign filers); GEV footnote-definition headline wart; STZ table-row wart; pending-filing cohort at 17; partial residuals at 5 (AFRM, LIN image-only; CCI no Q4-2024 8-K; SHOP foreign; SKYD pre-merger).

### Batch 103 — 6-K earnings-release discovery for foreign filers (2026-10-20)
Closes the carried "SHOP 6-K discovery" follow-up. SHOP's Q3-2024 quarter (2024-09-30) was the DB's last 6-K gap: its quarterly filing sits beyond the submissions feed's recent window (no paginated buckets), but the earnings release IS in the feed as a 6-K (`form6kq32024pressrelease.htm`, acc. 0001594805-24-000089). Three changes in `services/ir_download.py`: (1) `_extract_sec_filings` also promotes earnings-looking 6-Ks to period anchors — the primary-doc name must carry a quarter token for the 6-K's own reportDate quarter AND the reportDate must be that quarter's month-end (AGM/code-of-conduct/BFCM 6-Ks carry no quarter token; event-dated dividend 6-Ks are not month-ends). (2) `_extract_6ks` feeds every 6-K into the non-2.02 fallback pool (their `items` field is empty, so they can never enter the 2.02 pool). (3) `_match_8k_non202_fallback`: the 6-K's primary doc (the cover form, routinely press-release-named) is excluded from the exhibit pick — the real release was the oddly-named `a3pr.htm` EX-99.1, which `_pick_release_doc` would otherwise lose to the cover on name shape; the period reference is checked against the primary-doc name as well as the exhibit name (6-K exhibit names carry no period signal); equidistant candidates break ties toward the most release-named primary doc, so SHOP's press-release 6-K beats its same-day results-tables 6-K (whose 99.1 is financial statements, not the narrative release). `_release_name_rank` factored out of `_pick_release_doc`'s nested `_rank` for the tiebreaks; `_quarter_tokens` factored out of `_exhibit_matches_period`. Live backfill: SHOP 7/8 → 8/8 with the real Q3-2024 release ("Internet, Everywhere - November 12, 2024 ... 26% revenue growth and 19% free cash flow margin"). Strictly additive: domestic filers see zero behavior change (6-K anchors are dropped wherever a 10-Q/10-K covers the date; the fallback only runs when no 2.02 match exists).

**Tests:** 10 new (anchor filter incl. AGM/BFCM/dividend/annual-report negatives, anchor extraction, 6-K pool, 2.02-pool exclusion, primary-doc exclusion, pressrelease-vs-results tiebreak, unrelated-6-K rejection, end-to-end SHOP-shaped discovery, 10-Q shadowing). Full suite 881 green, `git diff --check` clean. Live: /company/SHOP, /company/SHOP/detail, /vs/AAPL, /tearsheet, /compare, / all 200; Q3 FY2024 detail renders the rescued release.

**Carried follow-ups:** GEV footnote-definition headline wart; STZ table-row wart; pending-filing cohort at 17; partial residuals at 4 (AFRM, LIN image-only; CCI no Q4-2024 8-K; SKYD pre-merger).

### Batch 104 — footnote-definition legend lines never become the headline (2026-10-20)
Closes the carried "GEV footnote-definition headline wart" follow-up. GEV's Workiva ex-99 opens with a footnote legend block — `1 Defined as remaining performance obligation (RPO)`, `*Non-GAAP Financial Measure`, `Page 1` — before the real headline, and the numeric definition line was picked as the headline (the real headline landed in the lede). **fix** (`data/filings.py`): new `_FOOTNOTE_DEF_RE` skips footnote-definition legend lines in both the headline pass and the lede pass — two narrow shapes: numeric key + definition verb (`1 Defined as ...`, `2 Represents ...`; the verb guards numeric-opening headlines like "2025 outlook raised" / "1Q26 record quarter" / "1 billion in buybacks", which never carry a definition verb) and glyph markers attached to the text (`*Non-GAAP Financial Measure`, `†Excludes ...`; a spaced `* Foo` is prose punctuation, not a legend). Also extended `_ADMIN_LINE_RE` with bare `^page\s+\d+$` ("Page 1" headers — GEV) and lone `^news$` (Southern Company's "News" section kicker, which sat before the contact block and would have become the headline once "Page 1" was skipped; a real headline is never one word). The `^news$` addition was forced by the blast-radius scan: SO's Q2-2024 exhibit carries the same Workiva header shape with a proper headline 10 lines down ("Southern Company reports second-quarter 2024 earnings") that the old code never reached — its stored headline was "Page 1". DB-wide scan: exactly 1 quarter with a footnote-def headline (GEV Q2 FY2025) and 2 with bare "Page 1" (GEV, SO); all others byte-identical. Live repair: cleared the two junk rows and re-ran the enrich — GEV Q2 FY2025 now leads with the real headline + "Second Quarter 2025 Highlights" financial bullets, SO Q2 FY2024 leads with its real headline. Dev-server lesson: `_enrich_press_release_highlights` sets attributes but never commits — the repair script's `db.refresh(row)` without a commit discarded the refill; commit before refresh.

**Tests:** 6 new (regex matches incl. glyph marker; headline-shape negatives incl. "2025 outlook raised", "1Q26 record quarter", "1 billion in buybacks", spaced-asterisk; GEV-shaped end-to-end; SO-shaped kicker end-to-end; NEWS CORP headline not swallowed by the `^news$` rule; lede-pass legend skip). Full suite 887 green, `git diff --check` clean. Live on :8123 (restarted for the new code): /company/GEV/detail, /company/SO/detail, /company/GEV, /company/SO, /calendar all 200; /company/GEV/detail/data?quarter=Q2 FY2025 renders the real headline + $12.4B-orders bullets with zero legend residue; /company/SO/detail/data?quarter=Q2 FY2024 renders the real headline. No template change (backend-only); screenshot gate N/A.

**Carried follow-ups:** STZ table-row wart (metric-table-as-highlights — real headline + real figures, a separate quality class, not junk); pending-filing cohort at 17; partial residuals at 4 (AFRM, LIN image-only; CCI no Q4-2024 8-K; SKYD pre-merger).

Seeding PGR/NKE/NFLX/CRM surfaced contact-block residue becoming stored headlines; a DB-wide scan found 248 affected quarters (~48 tickers), including ~200 stale pre-batch-97 `EX-99` rows. **Fixes** (`data/filings.py`): `_CONTACT_HEADER_RE` now covers `financial` role prefix (HSY), company-prefixed `Company Contacts` (PGR), bare role labels `Investor Relations`/`Corporate Communications`/`Media Relations` (AAL), colon-optional `Contacts?` covering bare `Media Contact`/`Investor Contact` (RTX) and inline-detail `Investor Contact 7930 Jones Branch Drive` (HLT), date-prefixed `July 29, 2026 Investor Contact:` (AWK); `_skip_contact_block` email/phone lookahead — a content-looking name/title line followed within 2 lines by email/phone is still contact detail (NKE, HSY, CSCO); headline pass skips masthead-residue run without company-name line when contact header follows (GD `11011 Sunset Hills Road`); `_ADMIN_LINE_RE` adds `^news from \S+$` kicker (AEP), `^the press release$` label (BAC), bare `^exhibit$` + `^\d+\.\d+$` for wrapped exhibit labels (WM/SHOO `EXHIBIT`/`99.1`); 1-word fragment rule joins (not skips) when next line is headline-shaped — `Dollar General Corporation Reports...`, `Plug Power Reports...`, `WM Announces...` (was too aggressive at ≤2 words, narrowed to ==1 after AWK `Aaron Musgrave`); company-name + headline-verb join (`Genuine Parts Company`/`Reports...`); leading page-number/URL run strip for page-image exhibits (F/Ford); page-line docs with no headline return None (BAC slide deck → honest "Not available"); `Earnings Release` label skip (MA, TOC-safe). Live repair: 280+ quarters re-extracted across 4 runs (182+98+fixes for bad-value regressions); DB now at 2005 highlights with the contact-residue cohort cleared.

**Tests:** 15 new (PGR/NKE/HSY/AAL/HLT/CSCO/RTX/GD/AEP/DG/F/AWK/GPC/CHD shapes). Full suite 902 green, `git diff --check` clean. Live: /company/PGR, /company/NKE, /company/MA, /company/WM, /company/SHOO all 200.

Fresh-ticker defect hunt. Seeded MRK, EMR, FCX, DOW, URI, CNC (8 quarters each, clean XBRL — no identity gaps, no inversions, FY labels consistent; DB at 258). Announces`, `Walmart Reports` (49 quarters, 9 tickers incl. WMT 7, LOPE 8, CTAS 8, MTD 8); **(2)** dash-wrapped page numbers as headlines — MRK `- 1 -`/`-`/`1 -`; **(3)** suite-address masthead residue — URI `100 First Stamford Place, Suite 700` (6 quarters); **(4)** split-kicker residue — MRK `Release` (from `News`/`Release` on separate lines), DE `,` (lone comma). **Fixes** (`data/filings.py`): `_HEADLINE_VERB_TAIL_RE` — a headline ending with a headline verb is a company+verb fragment, join the continuation (with the 140-char cap raised from 100 for Merck's 119-char continuation); `_join_wrapped_headline` now iterates (max 3) so multi-line headlines complete (`WM Announces`/`Fourth Quarter and`/`Full Year 2025 Results`); comma-wrap — a trailing comma is a wrap signal (`Merck & Co., Inc., Rahway, N.J.,`); `&`/`–`/`—` in the wrap-tail match without `\b` (which never fires between space and `&` — `Merck &` was the bug); `_ADMIN_LINE_RE` adds dash-page patterns (`^-\s*\d+\s*-$`, `^-\s*$`, `^\d+\s*-$`, `^-\s*\d+$`), bare `^release$`/`^press$`, lone `^,\s*$`; `_SUITE_ADDRESS_RE` for street addresses with suite designators in `_looks_like_masthead_residue`; trailing-comma strip on picked headlines. Live repair: 102 quarters re-extracted across 4 runs (58+42+2), all fixed; DB-wide scan confirms 0 remaining bad headlines across 2053 highlights. Closes the batch-105 `WMT fragment wraps` follow-up (7/7).

**Tests:** 14 new (dash-page, bare dash, page fragments, verb-fragment join + sentence-lede guard, long continuation, multi-join + bullet stop, suite-address + guard, split kicker, comma-wrap, ampersand-wrap, lone comma, trailing-comma strip). Full suite 916 green, `git diff --check` clean. Live: /company/MRK, /company/FCX, /company/URI, /company/WMT, /company/LOPE, /detail all 200; fixed headlines render.

### Batch 108 — round-37 seeds + ordinal-tail fragment defect hunt (2026-10-21)
Fresh-ticker defect hunt. Seeded XEL, PCG, SRE, PPL, PEG, ETR (8 quarters each, clean XBRL — no identity gaps, no inversions, FY labels consistent; DB at 270). The hunt surfaced 3 headline-fragment defect classes, all fixed in `data/filings.py`: **(1)** ordinal/period-word tail — a headline ending on First/Second/Third/Fourth/Full is a wrapped fragment (`_HEADLINE_ORDINAL_TAIL_RE`; "Plug Power Third" → "Plug Power Third Quarter 2025 Highlights", "Intercontinental Exchange Reports Strong Full" → "...Full Year 2025 Results", "Kroger Reports Fourth" → "...Fourth Quarter and Full-Year 2025 Results"); **(2)** "{ordinal} Quarter"/"Full Year" + bare-year continuation ("Intercontinental Exchange Reports Second Quarter" / "2026" — the year bypasses the digit-lead guard for this exact shape only); **(3)** fragment joins now continue through the wrap joiner — the 1-word ("Kroger"/"Reports Third Quarter 2025 Results and"/"Updates") and verb-lead joins broke immediately, storing dangling wrap tails ("...Results and"); they now feed the joined fragment into `_join_wrapped_headline`. Also fixed: `_skip_bare_masthead_name` misfired on "Plug Power Third" (read as bare masthead, "Quarter 2025 Highlights" as digit-residue, "$177 Million in Quarterly Revenue" picked as headline) — ordinal-tail candidates now return None (wrapped fragment, not masthead). ### Batch 112 — BRK.B no-headline design + dateline/quarter-label fixes (2026-10-22)
Design decision carried since batch 109, now resolved: Berkshire's earnings releases have **no headline at all** — company name + "NEWS RELEASE" + date + dateline, then the lede. The old extractor stored the company name ("BERKSHIRE HATHAWAY INC."); the pre-fix code picked the dateline ("Omaha, NE (BRK.A; BRK.B) –"). **Decision: a dateline is never a headline** — when the scan reaches a dateline with no headline found, the honest result is headline=None and the lede starts after the dateline. Two extractor fixes in `data/filings.py`: **(1)** new `_DATELINE_RE` (city + state/country, optional date/parenthetical tickers, trailing em/en dash or hyphen) checked at the generic headline-pick site — dateline reached → `body_start = i + 1`, break, headline stays None; **(2)** `_QUARTER_EARNINGS_LABEL_RE` generalizes the batch-110 "Earnings Release" label branch to quarter-prefixed variants ("FIRST QUARTER 2026 EARNINGS RELEASE" — Berkshire heads Q1 releases with it; the batch-110 branches only covered bare and single-token-prefixed labels, so the label became the headline on Q1 2026). Narrow: quarter-word + year + label words, so a genuine "X Schedules First Quarter Earnings Release" headline never matches. (Caught a name collision mid-batch: `_QUARTER_LABEL_RE` already existed for "Q1 2025" labels — renamed to `_QUARTER_EARNINGS_LABEL_RE`.) **Repaired BRK.B ×8** — all verified against live ex-99.1 exhibits before writing; a second pass confirmed 8/8 idempotent skips. Two exhibit forensics on the way: (a) four quarters' 8-Ks exist in EDGAR but `discover_earnings_filings`' item-2.02 filter misses them (exhibits located directly); (b) the 2025-03-31 seed had matched the *wrong* same-day 8-K — the Buffett-succession announcement (0001193125-25-115695) instead of the real earnings release (0001193125-25-113900) — repaired to the correct exhibit.

**Tests:** 3 new (dateline-never-headline end-to-end, quarter-prefixed label end-to-end, dateline/label regex narrowness negatives). Full suite 957 green, `git diff --check` clean. Blast-radius: old-vs-new extraction on 18 good exhibits via git stash — 0 diffs across 36 quarters (both fixes). Live: /company/BRK.B + /company/BRK.B/detail 200; detail page renders the clean lede, 0 dateline occurrences.

### Batch 113 — earnings-8-K matching: post-filing grace pool + 75-day annual window (2026-10-22)
Resolved the batch-112 forensic follow-up: the four "missed" BRK.B earnings 8-Ks DO carry Item 2.02 — the batch-97 guards dropped them, not the item filter. Berkshire files the 10-Q/10-K a day or two BEFORE furnishing the earnings-release 8-K (Q1'25: 10-Q 2025-05-05, release 2025-05-06; Q1'26: 2026-05-04 → 2026-05-07; Q2'26: 2026-08-10 → 2026-08-11), violating the "earnings must predate the formal filing" guard; and the FY-2025 release came 61 days after year-end (2025-12-31 → 2026-03-02), outside the 60-day quarterly window. Two fixes in `services/ir_download.py`, shared by `_match_8k` and the batch-101 non-2.02 fallback via the new `_er_8k_candidate_pools` helper: **(1)** post-filing grace pool — 2.02 8-Ks furnished 1–3 days AFTER the formal filing fill quarters with no primary candidate (so PNC's 2025-11-07 investor-conference 8-K, 4 days post-filing with a genuine pre-filing 2.02 in play, still cannot win); **(2)** annual (10-K) periods get a 75-day period-end window (61-day BRK.B release now qualifies; the next quarter's earnings start ~100 days out, so no cross-period capture). All 8 BRK.B quarters now discover the exact accessions the batch-112 manual forensic located directly — future seeds/backfills get them automatically. The same-day multi-8-K follow-up (seed picked the succession 8-K) is closed as a seed-script artifact, not a production defect: the succession 8-K carries 5.02,9.01 (no 2.02) so it can never enter the discovery candidate pool.

Full suite 962 green, `git diff --check` clean. Seed round 38: APO, ETN, CMG, ON, KKR, BIIB — 8 clean quarters each through the production path; DB at **276 companies**. The 53.7% was TTM *net-income* growth (the `growth_is_fallback` path), and the PEG path was never reachable (it requires per-share EPS) — so the batch-74 reroute story was doubly false. Fix in `services/valuation.py`: the batch-74 hyper-growth opening line now requires `ttm_eps_diluted is not None`; no-EPS filers get the honest "No per-share earnings to anchor a P/E multiple — valuing on sales instead (less reliable)." line. Verified the no-EPS cohort: BKR/HSY/V/LEN already rendered the honest line (growth ≤40%); OXY is Watch/unavailable; ON keeps the batch-74 line correctly (real EPS growth 41.3%, 8/8 quarters with EPS data). The sales-fallback valuation itself (Strong Buy +35.8% on real revenue/margin/net-income, Medium confidence) is the designed behavior — only the copy was wrong.

Live: /company/{KKR,STZ,ON} all 200 with the corrected line rendering (dev server restarted to pick up the code change — the running process had the old module loaded). ### Batch 115 — residual junk-headline sweep: never-headline guard + wrapped-fragment contact skip (2026-10-22)
A DB-wide scan (batch-107 script) found **40 quarters** whose stored headline was junk the earlier batches never classified — and the current extractor had quietly made several *worse* (ROST's rule → a contact title, WELL's quote → a contact line, VZ's filename → a dateline fragment). Forensics (monkeypatched branch tracing on the live exhibits) found the true paths: **(1)** lone-punctuation lines (`_`, `'`, `` ` ``, `\`) were misread as *bare masthead names*, jumping the pick onto a contact-detail line; **(2)** `_skip_contact_block` ate Sempra's 3-word wrapped-headline fragment ("Sempra Reports Third-Quarter" / "2025 Results"); **(3)** image-filename tokens ('VZQTR20FIN'), page-header artifacts ('1 §:)airbnb'), exchange:ticker masthead lines ('NYSE: TRV'), and colon section labels ('Summary Points:') had no guard. Fixes in `data/filings.py`: `_NEVER_HEADLINE_RE` (no-alnum lines, 5+-char single alnum tokens with a digit, page-number + junk-token, exchange:ticker, 1–4-word colon labels — narrowness-tested against '3M', '2026 Outlook', real headlines); `_skip_contact_block` stops at a wrapped-headline fragment (`_looks_like_wrapped_headline_start`: wrap/verb/ordinal tail + plausible continuation, phone-bearing continuations excluded); `_HEADLINE_ORDINAL_TAIL_RE`/`_HEADLINE_QUARTER_TAIL_RE` cover hyphenated "Third-Quarter" and `_HEADLINE_YEAR_LEAD_RE` covers year-led continuations ("2025 Results"); picked headlines get bullet markers stripped (Palantir's "• U.S. revenue grew ..." rows); `_looks_like_masthead_residue` recognizes exchange:ticker lines. **Live repair: 58 rows re-extracted** (ROST 8, TFC 8, VZ 5, TRV 8 — including 7 more '485 Lexington Avenue' address-line headlines the scan missed, SRE 4, XYZ 7, SKYD, WELL, FICO, DE, CVX, ABNB, PLTR 3), **1 cleared** (PCAR 2026-03-31 — no ex-99 exhibit, verified batch 109; stale 'PACCAR Inc' removed so the UI shows "Not available"). Post-repair scan: **0 suspicious headlines** across 2188 quarters.

**Tests:** 10 new (lone-punctuation, underscore-rule + contact titles, image-filename token, exchange:ticker, section-label + bullet headline, page-header junk, contact-block wrapped fragment, hyphenated quarter-tail join, bullet strip, never-headline narrowness). ### Batch 117 — pending-filing cohort recheck + note-label fix: backward quarter walk (2026-10-24)
Rechecked the full pending-filing cohort (now **26 companies**, up from 22 — new seeds added BIIB and others): every member's Q2 FY2026 10-Q revenue facts are still absent from live EDGAR companyfacts (verified per ticker via the app's own EdgarClient; older quarters all present), so all 26 notes are genuine lag, none stale — no notes cleared. Two members had moved on: **DOW** (Q3 10-Q filed Oct 23) and **URI** (Oct 21) have ingested Q3 facts, and visiting their pages pulled Q3 into the DB — but the note then mislabeled the still-missing quarter as **"Q4 FY2026 results (10-Q filed Jul 24, 2026)"**. Root cause in `services/financials.py`: `_quarter_label_for_report_date` passed `(newest_end, report_date)` to the forward-only `_quarter_steps` (`max(1, ...)` floor, built for the xbrl sequence repair which always passes earlier < later and negates explicitly) — for a middle-gap filing predating the newest row, the negative distance floored to +1 and walked Q3 forward to Q4 instead of back to Q2. Fix signs the steps at the call site (forward via `_quarter_steps`, backward via negated `_quarter_steps`, same-end returns the newest label), leaving the shared helper untouched; `_walk_label` already walks predecessors for negative steps. **Live repair: DOW/URI notes recomputed on next page view to the correct "Q2 FY2026 … showing Q3 FY2026"** (server restarted to load the fix). Notable data finding: EDGAR has ingested both companies' Q3 10-Q facts while their Q2 10-Q facts remain absent — Q3 landing does not backfill Q2, so the middle-gap note is the honest state until EDGAR ingests Q2 or the extractor gains a YTD-difference derivation (open question, not built here).

**Tests:** 4 new (backward walk, backward across year boundary, same-end label, middle-gap note end-to-end asserting "Q2 FY2026" present and "Q4 FY2026" absent). Announces" / "2024 Results", and `_join_wrapped_headline`'s digit-led bad-start guard refused the join, stranding "2024 Results" as the first body line — which killed the leading-bullet-block detection, let a "Financial Highlights" table header become the whole body (<200 chars), and returned None. Fix in `data/filings.py`: the digit guard is bypassed for the exact shape verb-tail headline + `^20\d{2}\s+Results\b` (a "YYYY Results" line is never a lede start; a bare year after a verb tail still does not join). **(2)** Dateline sentence before bullets: Prudential's other quarters put the dateline sentence ("NEWARK, N.J. – ... today reported ...") between headline and bullets, so `_leading_bullet_block` returned None and the table header (or the dateline prose) became the body — 2025 Q4 and 2026 Q2 stored the "(in millions, unaudited)" table header as highlights, 2026 Q1 stored the dateline paragraph. Fix: `_leading_bullet_block` skips a leading `_DATELINE_LEAD_RE` line (all-caps location + dash, never bullet material) before any bullet is collected. **Live repair: PRU 8/8** — 2024 Q4 filled via backfill, 2025 Q4 / 2026 Q1 / 2026 Q2 re-extracted and overwritten (new-value-differs-and-non-None discipline); all 8 quarters now carry bullet bodies.

Full suite 985 green, `git diff --check` clean. **Blast-radius:** full-text old-vs-new extraction (git stash) on 16 tickers × 130 quarters (AAPL/MSFT/JPM/XOM/BRK.B/TSLA/PNC/C/DUOL/GS + all 6 new tickers) — exactly 4 diffs, all PRU, all improvements; the other 126 quarters byte-identical. ### Batch 121 — derivation-vintage rule: a restated annual must not pair with an unrestated 9M + DB-wide repair (2026-10-24)
Closes the batch-120 carried follow-up: OXY's stored Q4 FY2024 revenue ($2,054M) was a mixed-basis derivation — the 2025 10-K restated FY2024 Revenues $26,725M → $22,019M (ex-OxyChem) while the 2024 Q3 10-Q's 9M fact stayed $19,965M, so latest-FY − 9M fabricated Q4. New rule in `data/xbrl.py` (`_own_tenk_annual_value` + `_own_ytd_value` + `_values_differ`, applied in `_derive_q4_income` and the cash-flow un-cumulation): when a later filing restated the year's annual fact AND the 9M fact was not restated, derive Q4 from the contemporaneous pair (own-10-K FY − 9M 10-Q) — OXY Q4 FY2024 now derives $6,760M, the as-reported figure. The refinement matters: substituting unconditionally would mix bases the other way when the 9M was restated too — AAPL FY2013 (annual 40.03 → 5.72 AND 9M 31.67 → 4.52, both post-split) keeps its latest-pair derivation (Q4 EPS ~1.19), while AAPL FY2012 (annual restated, 9M never restated) gains its previously coherence-blocked Q4 (EPS 2.188 on the series' only-4:1-adjusted basis — the filed Q4 stub value, relabeled from FY to Q4). Split-rounding noise is fenced by `_values_differ` (values agreeing at the coarser filing precision count as the same; blind spot bounded at ≤5¢ for EPS, $0 for integer dollars). Break years still abstain first via the batch-85/120 guard, which runs before this rule.

**Tests:** 8 new (OXY contemporaneous-pair revenue end-to-end; cash-flow pair; both-restated-keeps-latest EPS + cash-flow; annual-restated-only EPS; split-rounding unit + end-to-end; no-own-10-K unchanged). Full suite 1005 green, `git diff --check` clean. **Blast-radius:** full DB-wide old-vs-new derived-Q4 pass over all 292 companies (revenue, NI, EPS basic/diluted, OpCF; fp-label noise separated from value changes): 757 value diffs across 220 tickers + 10 added/removed derived rows + 25 label-only stub relabels (FY→Q4, values identical). The other 749 diffs sit on ends outside stored windows and self-correct on the next seed of each ticker. ### Batch 123 — seed round 42 + contact-table headline fix: flattened two-column contact tables are never headlines (2026-10-25)
Seed round 42 through the production path: **LULU, WDC, FSLR, ENPH, TTD, AKAM** are new. DB at **298 companies**. Quarter sequences clean, including LULU's January fiscal year-end (FY2024Q4 = 2025-02-02) and WDC's June year-end with a fresh FY2027Q1 (2026-10-02). Disclosure backfill: **8/8 highlights for all 6 tickers.** Seeding surfaced one real defect: **AKAM's two Q4 quarters had contact names as headlines** — Akamai's full-year (Q4) releases put the contact table before the title while Q1–Q3 put the title first, so both Q4 quarters stored "Christine Simeone Mark Stoutenberg" (2024) and "Johanna Schmitt Mark Stoutenberg" (2025), with the role row, doubled company row, and real wrapped headline polluting the body. Root cause in `data/filings.py`: the names row is 4 words and headline-shaped, so no existing skip caught it — there is no contact-header line for the header branch (the roles row is a doubled label, not a header), and `_skip_bare_masthead_name` requires ≤3 words and a non-headline-shaped line. Fix: `_skip_contact_table` — when a line is followed by a role-label row (single or doubled), is short (≤6 words) with no terminal punctuation and no headline word, and a headline-shaped line appears within 8 lines past table-continuation rows (repeated phrase, company-name line, admin/contact line, short label), the table is skipped and the loop picks the real headline, which then joins normally ("AKAMAI REPORTS FOURTH QUARTER 2024 AND" / "FULL-YEAR 2024 FINANCIAL RESULTS"). `_CONTACT_HEADER_RE` also gains the doubled-role-label alternative ("Media Relations Investor Relations") so the shape reads as a contact header on every path that uses it. **Live repair: both AKAM Q4 rows re-extracted** — headlines now the real wrapped titles with their bullet blocks intact; the other 6 AKAM quarters were already correct and are unchanged.

**Tests:** 7 new (AKAM Q4 end-to-end; doubled-role header match; repeated-phrase unit; table-skip index; verb-headline negative; no-headline-ahead negative; no-role-row negative). Full suite 1015 green, `git diff --check` clean. **Blast-radius:** direct re-extraction (not backfill, which skips filled rows) over AKAM + AAPL/ROK/MTB/WDC/FSLR — exactly 2 attributable diffs, both AKAM Q4 improvements; ROK/MTB/WDC/FSLR identical; AAPL's 8 diffs are pre-existing 1-char whitespace drift (stored rows predate the current `_html_to_text` spacing — proven by re-extracting with the pre-123 extractor via stash, which also yields len 1395 vs stored 1396), left untouched. A DB-wide scan for the defect shape (stored body opening with a role-label row) across all 2370 quarters with highlights found exactly the 2 AKAM rows — the defect is fully contained. ### Batch 124 — compound earnings-release document title skipped: LULU Q3 headlines repaired (2026-10-25)
Closes the batch-123 carried follow-up: **LULU's Q3 FY2024 and Q3 FY2025 headlines were the 8-K document title "EARNINGS RELEASE & STOCK REPURCHASE PROGRAM" instead of the real release title.** Raw-document investigation showed the shape: lululemon's Q3 releases combine the earnings release with a stock-repurchase announcement in one 8-K, so the exhibit's document title sits before "Exhibit 99.1" while the real headline follows it ("LULULEMON ATHLETICA INC. ANNOUNCES THIRD QUARTER FISCAL 2024/2025 RESULTS"). Q4 was fine because its title is the bare "EARNINGS RELEASE", which the label branch already skips — the compound form was the unhandled case. Fix in `data/filings.py`: `_is_compound_earnings_title` — when a line carries "earnings release" followed by "&" or "and", an exhibit-marker line ("Exhibit 99.1" / "EX-99.1", new `_EXHIBIT_MARKER_RE`) appears within the next 2 lines, and the line after the marker is a headline-shaped line carrying a headline verb (announces/reports), the title line is skipped and the loop picks the real headline. The guards are each load-bearing: the "&"/"and" requirement keeps bare labels on their existing branch; the marker lookahead keeps a compound title with no marker after it on old behavior; and the verb-headline requirement is what keeps JPMorgan's stored headline safe — its "EARNINGS RELEASE - THIRD QUARTER 2025 RESULTS" title is also followed by an exhibit marker, but the line after the marker is the company name/address block, not a verb headline. A first version of the title regex carried `\b` after `&`, which can never match ("&" followed by a space has no word boundary) — caught by the new tests, fixed to `(&|and\b)`. **Live repair: both LULU Q3 rows re-extracted** — headlines now the real titles, bodies byte-identical apart from the headline itself (+25 chars each); the other 6 LULU quarters unchanged.

**Tests:** 4 new (LULU Q3 end-to-end; "&" and "and" compound forms; helper guard negatives — no marker, prose after marker, bare label, JPM dash-title; JPM-shaped doc keeps its headline). Full suite 1019 green, `git diff --check` clean. **Blast-radius:** direct re-extraction over LULU + JPM/XOM/AKAM/AAPL/ROK/MTB/WDC/FSLR — exactly 2 attributable diffs, both LULU Q3 headline improvements; XOM/AKAM/ROK/MTB/WDC/FSLR identical; JPM's 8 body diffs and AAPL's 8 1–2-char diffs are pre-existing drift (proven by re-extracting JPM with the pre-124 extractor via stash — byte-identical to post-124 output — and by batch 123's AAPL proof), left untouched. A DB-wide scan for the defect shape (stored headline containing "earnings release" with "&"/"and") across all 2370 quarters with highlights found exactly the 2 LULU rows — fully contained. Live: /company/{LULU,JPM,XOM,AKAM} overview + detail 200, quarter-specific detail/data renders for both repaired Q3 quarters show the real headlines with zero doc-title remnants.

### Batch 125 — slide-deck bullet-run mega-lines split: BAC 8/8 blank quarters repaired (2026-10-25)
A DB-wide blank-highlights audit (25 of 2394 quarters) found **BAC blank on all 8 quarters** — the only whole-company blank left after batches 92–94. Discovery was fine (all 8 quarters resolve to `bacMMDDYYYYex991.htm` exhibits); the extractor returned None. Root cause: Bank of America's releases are slide decks, and `_html_to_text` flattens a whole highlights slide into a single 7,000-char line with bullets joined by " • " and no line breaks. The headline loop saw one line over the 300-char cap, picked no headline, and the batch-105 page-line guard (no headline + first paragraph > 1000 chars) discarded the release — the exact shape batch 105 deliberately left as "Not available". Fix in `data/filings.py`: `_split_bullet_runs`, applied at the top of `extract_press_release_highlights` before any headline/section/lede logic — a line longer than the headline cap with at least two " • " separators splits into one pseudo-line per bullet, with continuation pieces keeping a "• " prefix so downstream bullet logic (lede bullets, the batch-115 bullet-headline strip) treats them as bullets. Short lines and single-separator lines are untouched. The split's first piece is the deck title ("1 2Q26 Financial Highlights… Business Segment Highlights… Consumer Banking"), so the headline loop also gains a never-headline branch for slide-deck titles (page number + quarter token + "Financial Highlights") — without it the title piece became the headline. Result for BAC Q2 2026: headline "Net income of $3.3 billion" (lead bullet, Palantir precedent — the true "Bank of America Reports…" headline is glued to sub-heads and endnotes inside the final bullet piece with no sentence boundary, so exact recovery would need BAC-specific surgery and is deliberately not attempted) with genuine segment bullets as the body. The bullet-as-headline behavior is load-bearing for Palantir/Skydance tests, so no bullet skip was added to the headline loop. **Live repair: all 8 BAC quarters backfilled** — every quarter now shows real Consumer Banking segment bullets (e.g. Q2 2026: net income $3.3B, revenue $11.3B up 5%); blank cohort drops 25 → 17.

**Tests:** 3 new (mega-line split unit behavior + BAC-shaped end-to-end extraction; deck-title never-headline narrowness). Full suite 1021 green, `git diff --check` clean. **Blast-radius:** the split only fires on >300-char lines with ≥2 " • " separators, so prose releases are byte-identical by construction; the never-headline branch only matches lines starting with a page number + token + "Financial Highlights". Live: /company/BAC overview 200, /company/BAC/detail/data 200 rendering the backfilled "Net income of $3.3 billion" highlights.

**Carried follow-ups:** STZ table-row wart (deliberate); CB "Core" 5-line wraps; ABNB heading-less metric table; CINF/MTB cosmetic prefixes; XOM doc-title headlines (title-after-marker shape, needs its own rule); NEW: EOG is the next systemic blank cohort (7/8 quarters) — a different defect class from BAC: its exhibit is a combined release + supplement packet whose "Table of Contents" + supplement TOC entries are not skipped by the SPG TOC rule, so extraction lands on footnote fragments; needs its own TOC/supplement investigation; SKYD 4 blank (structurally unextractable per batch 48) + PEG 2 + AFRM/CCI/LIN/PCAR 1 each; pending-filing cohort at 28 per today's count (was 26 + VRSK Q2 2026); Wikipedia 403s; "Financial Highlights" table-header sections winning when a release has no bullets at all; the batch-121 transient-window limitation.

### Batch 126 — supplement-only packets are not highlights: EOG's footnote/table garbage becomes honest blanks (2026-10-25)
Closes the batch-125 EOG follow-up, with a corrected mechanism: EOG's exhibits are combined release + supplement packets whose narrative release text lives in full-page JPEG images (7–12 page images per exhibit); the text layer holds only the supplement TOC and tables. The batch-125 note's "TOC entries not skipped" diagnosis was imprecise — the actual paths are twofold. (1) Six exhibits land on a table footnote definition as the headline: the SPG TOC rule over-consumes (every table row is ≤140 chars, and table footers supply the ≥2 page lines), stopping at the first >140-char line, which is a footnote legend — e.g. Q2 2026's "(B)Crude oil and condensate volumes are from UAE and Bahrain operations..." with a Balance Sheets table as the body; Q1 FY2026 stored exactly this shape. (2) Q3 FY2024 lands on the TOC subtitle ("Supplemental Financial and Operating Data Page") with the TOC entries as the body, because its TOC has only one standalone page line so the TOC rule's ≥2-page requirement never fires. Fix in `data/filings.py`, two result-level guards after headline cleanup (deliberately NOT a headline-loop skip — skipping footnote lines in the loop would just move the pick onto a table heading or financial row, since a 700-line table document always yields some pick): a footnote-definition headline (single letter/digit in parentheses, `_FOOTNOTE_HEADLINE_RE`) returns None, and a body consisting entirely (≥2 lines) of title + page-number lines (`_TOC_ENTRY_RE`) returns None. The single-character bound is load-bearing: "(NYSE: ACME)" and "(2026)" parentheticals are not footnote markers and keep working (both covered by tests). The image-page narrative itself is structurally unextractable (same class as SKYD per batch 48) — no OCR is attempted; honest blank beats fabricated highlights. **Live repair: EOG Q1 FY2026's stored footnote-table row cleared to NULL** (highlights + source); all 8 EOG exhibits now extract to None, so the blank cohort goes 17 → 18 with EOG honestly 8/8 blank instead of 7 blank + 1 garbage.

**Tests:** 5 new (footnote-headline end-to-end; TOC-subtitle/TOC-body end-to-end; NYSE-prefixed and year-prefixed headline preservation; normal release with numbered lede lines preserved). Full suite 1026 green, `git diff --check` clean. **Blast-radius:** DB-wide scans over all 2378 stored highlight rows — exactly 1 footnote-headline row (EOG Q1, cleared) and 0 TOC-entry bodies, so the guards change no other stored row; post-fix live re-extraction over all 8 EOG exhibits returns None on every quarter. Live: /company/EOG overview + detail 200 with the Q1 FY2026 garbage gone.

**Carried follow-ups:** STZ table-row wart (deliberate); CB "Core" 5-line wraps; ABNB heading-less metric table; CINF/MTB cosmetic prefixes; XOM doc-title headlines (title-after-marker shape, needs its own rule); EOG now honestly 8/8 blank (image-page narrative, structurally unextractable — no further extractor work will recover it short of OCR); SKYD 4 blank (structurally unextractable per batch 48) + PEG 2 + AFRM/CCI/LIN/PCAR 1 each; pending-filing cohort at 28 + VRSK Q2 2026; Wikipedia 403s; "Financial Highlights" table-header sections winning when a release has no bullets at all; the batch-121 transient-window limitation; the `_skip_toc` over-consumption itself (it still eats EOG's tables before the guards catch the outcome — bounded fix left open, since the result-level guards now make its over-consumption harmless).

The verb guard is load-bearing: a release whose only title is the quarter label keeps old behavior instead of being skipped into bullets. **Live repair: all 8 XOM quarters re-extracted** — headlines now the real titles (e.g. Q3 2024 "ExxonMobil Announces Third-Quarter 2024 Results"), bodies byte-identical apart from the headline itself (proven by the headline-swap equality check, not assumed).

### Batch 128 — seed round 43 + 991-date exhibit convention: CTSH's 8 blank quarters repaired (2026-10-25)
Seed round 43 through the production path: **USB, FTNT, CTSH, MO, PH** are new (8 quarters each, zero nulls in revenue/net income/diluted EPS). DB at **303 companies**. BK did not seed — verified retired, not a pipeline defect: EDGAR's ticker map has no BK (KeyError on lookup), and BNY Mellon already sits in the DB as BNY per batch 78. Disclosure backfill: **8/8 highlights for FTNT, MO, PH, USB**; CTSH 0/8 — the round's defect. Root cause in `services/ir_download.py`: Cognizant names its 99.1 exhibit `exhibit9916302026.htm` (99.1 + quarter-end date 6/30/2026, no separator), and `_release_name_rank` matched no rule for it — the tier-0 first regex's `(?!\d|\.\d)` guard rejects it because the month digit follows the 99.1 "1" directly, and the "991" infix lookahead had no date-run alternative, so `_find_ex99_doc` returned None on all 8 quarters even though the 2.02 8-Ks were matched. The exhibit itself extracts cleanly (real headline + bullets, proven on the live Q2 2026 file before any code change). Fix: the "991" infix lookahead gains `\d{6,8}($|[-_.])` — a 6–8-digit date run (MDDYYYY or MMDDYYYY) directly after 991. The guards are load-bearing in both directions: 99.2/99.3 supplements (`exhibit992/9936302026.htm`) never match (they carry 992/993, not 991), and `doc9912final.htm` stays unmatched (its digit run is 1 digit, not 6–8; a 5-digit run is likewise excluded). **Live repair: CTSH 8/8 backfilled** — real headlines on every quarter (e.g. Q2 2024 "Cognizant Reports Second Quarter 2024 Results"), and the detail/data route renders the latest stored quarter's repaired highlights.

Full suite 1032 green, `git diff --check` clean. **Blast-radius:** all 84 ir_download tests green (every prior exhibit convention still picks correctly); the rank change fires only on "991" + 6–8-digit-date names, so no previously-matching filename changes tier. ### Batch 130 — deck-title page skipped: USB's 8 deck-section headlines repaired (2026-10-26)
Closes the batch-128/129 USB follow-up. Root cause in `data/filings.py`: U.S. Bancorp's exhibits are slide decks headed by a title page, "<NQ>YY Key Financial Data" (e.g. "3Q24 Key Financial Data"), with the highlights section heading ("<NQ>YY (Financial) Highlights") immediately after it — and the headline loop picked the title page because no skip matched it (batch 125's deck-title rule covers only BAC's page-number-prefixed "1 2Q26 Financial Highlights" shape). Fix, two parts: (1) a headline-loop branch before the never-headline check — when the title line is followed by a highlights heading (≤120 chars), the heading becomes the headline and the body starts AT the heading, so the section extractor still finds its content; (2) the title shape joins `_NEVER_HEADLINE_RE` as a backstop for decks where no highlights heading follows. The body_start-at-heading is load-bearing: a naive skip (headline = heading, body after it) loses the section anchor, and the body would fall back to a later business section, losing the financial bullets — the naive version was traced, not assumed, and rejected on that ground. **Live repair: all 8 USB quarters re-extracted** — headlines now the section headings (e.g. Q2 2026 "2Q26 Financial Highlights"), bodies byte-identical on every quarter (asserted per row, not assumed).

### Batch 134 — subsidiary-list masthead skipped: CINF's 9 subsidiary-line headlines repaired (2026-10-26)
Closes the CINF follow-up carried from batch 120 and deferred in batch 133. Root cause in `data/filings.py`: Cincinnati Financial heads each exhibit with a flattened subsidiary table — one line per table row, " n " joining the columns ("The Cincinnati Insurance Company n The Cincinnati Indemnity Company", etc.) — ahead of the contact block and the real headline. The headline loop picked the second row: the first row (71 chars) enters the company-name masthead branch, but `_skip_masthead_block` stops at the second row (not masthead residue), and the second row (90 chars) escapes that branch's 80-char bound, so it was picked as the headline — dragging the remaining rows, the real headline, and the dateline into the 6-line lede (and, on section-body quarters, stranding the real headline out of the stored text entirely). Fix: `_is_subsidiary_list` — a headline-loop skip for lines that split on " n " into ≥2 segments, every segment ending with a company suffix (via `_COMPANY_NAME_LINE_RE`) and within 80 chars. The suffix-every-segment rule is the whole discriminator: a prose line containing " n " fails it, and a real headline never consists solely of " n "-joined company names. **Live repair: all 9 stored CINF rows re-extracted** — headlines now the real titles (e.g. Q3 2026 "Cincinnati Financial Reports Third-Quarter 2026 Results"). Bodies: byte-identical on the 4 section-body quarters (Q1 2025, Q1/Q2/Q3 2026 — the "Investment and Balance Sheet Highlights" section content, genuine, kept as-is); strictly better on the 5 lede quarters (subsidiary rows + duplicated headline out, full opening bullet set in — Q4 2024 goes from 2 bullets to 5). One probe artifact worth recording: the first CINF probe read the 2026-09-30 stored row as None because a `datetime.date` period key does not match the stored "YYYY-MM-DD" string under the default sqlite3 adapter — the repair script normalizes period keys to strings, and the row was present and repaired like the rest.

**Tests:** 2 new (CINF Q4 end-to-end; guard negatives — single company name, prose " n " line, one non-company segment, genuine headline, over-long segment). Full suite 1044 green, `git diff --check` clean. **Blast-radius:** DB-wide scan over all 2425 stored highlight rows for a subsidiary-list line found exactly the 9 CINF rows — fully contained, post-repair scan finds 0; old-vs-new extraction via `git stash` over 48 quarters (AAPL, MSFT, JPM, MTB, XOM, CHD) shows 0 diffs, so the skip touches nothing outside CINF. Live: /company/CINF overview + detail + detail/data 200, detail/data rendering the repaired Q3 2026 headline with zero " n "-joined subsidiary remnants. (Server note: the dev server was restarted mid-batch to pick up the fixed extractor module; the pre-restart 000 was startup lag, not a route defect.)

### Batch 135 — fragment-tail headline wraps: CB's 8 stored fragment headlines repaired (2026-10-27)
Closes the CB "Core" follow-up carried from batch 120 (and deferred through batch 134). Root cause in `data/filings.py`: Chubb's release headline is a whole paragraph wrapped at arbitrary points, and the wrap lands mid-phrase on a word no existing tail rule recognized — a modifier awaiting its head noun ("Net Income and Core" / "Operating Income", "P&C Up 10.6% and Life" / "Insurance", "Respectively; Consolidated" / "Net Premiums Written", "P&C Combined" / "Ratio", "Excluding Catastrophe Losses and Prior" / "Period Development") or the preposition "Up" awaiting its figure ("Net Income of $7.35, Up" / "34.6%") — and digit-led percentage continuations ("21.7%;", "85.2%;") were refused outright by the digit-led bad-start guard, stranding the figure as the first body line. The old joiner stored only the first line ("Chubb Reports Second Quarter Per Share Net Income and Core") and left the rest of the headline paragraph as body lines. Fix: `_HEADLINE_FRAGMENT_TAIL_RE` (Core/Record/Consolidated/Life/Net/Operating/Combined/Prior/P&C/Up — each a word that cannot end a complete headline), `_HEADLINE_PCT_LEAD_RE` (percentage-led continuations bypass the digit guard only after a fragment or wrap tail — never after a verb/ordinal tail, where a digit-led line is a metric-table row), and the join cap rises 3 → 12 (CB's longest paragraph needs 10 joins; every join still requires a trigger plus all continuation guards). One guard against the new tail's weakest case: a fragment-tail join is blocked when the continuation is itself a fresh headline — Danaher's cover slogan "Innovation at the speed of life" ends on "life" but is followed by "Danaher Reports … Results", and joining them would build a Franken-headline (the guard was proven by a pre-guard run that produced exactly that on all 12 DHR quarters; post-guard DHR is byte-identical). **Live repair: all 8 stored CB rows re-extracted** — 6 now carry the full headline paragraph (e.g. Q2 2024, 58 → 418 chars; Q4 2025, 60 → 525 chars), 2 partial: Q3 2024 stops at "…Net Investment Income" (the "Both Up Double-Digit…" lines stay in the body — nothing lost), Q4 2024 stops at "…and Life Insurance" (its tail clause "Up 15.7%; P&C Combined Ratio of 86.6%" drops out of the stored text: the orphan line between the headline paragraph and the bullets is dropped by the section extractor — a future "Insurance"-tail rule recovers it into the headline; the trade buys the full bullet body, which the old 6-line lede never reached).

**Tests:** 4 new (CB Q2 2024 end-to-end full-paragraph join; "Up" + percentage-continuation chain; guard negatives — sentence-terminated lede after a fragment tail, digit-led line after a verb tail, bullet after a fragment tail, complete "Record Growth" headline untouched; DHR slogan fresh-headline block + Merck wrap-comma exemption). Full suite 1047 green, `git diff --check` clean. **Blast-radius:** DB-wide scan over all 2425 stored highlight rows for fragment-tail headlines found exactly 20 — CB 8 (all repaired, post-repair scan finds 0 CB), DHR 8 (slogan, unchanged by design), ABNB 4 (table-fragment headlines from the shareholder-letter path, untouched — the joiner never runs there); old-vs-new extraction via `git stash` over 144 quarters (CB, DHR, ABNB, AAPL, MSFT, JPM, MTB, XOM, CHD, CINF, LULU, AKAM) shows exactly 12 diffs, all CB headline improvements, 0 elsewhere. Live: /company/CB overview + detail + detail/data 200, detail/data rendering the repaired Q1 2026 full headline with zero fragment remnants.

**Carried follow-ups:** STZ table-row wart (deliberate); CB partial residuals — Q3 2024 "Both Up Double-Digit…" wrap (continuation starts with "Both", no tail rule fires) and Q4 2024 "Insurance" → "Up 15.7%…" wrap (an "Insurance"-tail rule would recover the dropped tail clause into the headline); ABNB heading-less metric table (shareholder-letter metric parse needs a highlights heading); EOG honestly 8/8 blank (image-page narrative, structurally unextractable — no further extractor work will recover it short of OCR); SKYD 4 blank (structurally unextractable per batch 48) + PEG 2 + AFRM/CCI/LIN/PCAR 1 each; pending-filing cohort at 29 (re-verified live batch 132 — zero landings, genuinely still EDGAR lag, not stale notes); partial residuals incl.
